# QHCF Carrion Fields map reference

Built 2026-10-19T22:28:45.943Z. 169 verified map transcriptions; 1 bounded candidates remain missing.

These are map transcriptions, not validated playable routes. Do not infer movement from spacing, labels, + counts, crossings, doors, boundaries, or special exits.

## A Northern Trail

Status: Current capture
Source: http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=A_Northern_Trail&oldid=4962
Source/capture date: This page was last modified on 8 October 2026, at 23:48.

```text
The Northern Trails was created by Uller, July 2001.  Use for CarrionFields only please.

 P - Path in the Plains
 R - A Path Along a Ridgeline
 F - A Path in the Foothills
 B - Bridge over Nanthor
 R - Deep Ravine
 U - Under a brigde on river Nanthor
 N - River Nanthor
 H - A Hidden Path in the Plains
```

```text
      mine entrance (4)

S - Shrieker mushroom (30 hours deafen)                            
Y - Yellow Fungi (30 hours insanity)                               
W - Giant purple worm (swallows people) (wandering) [random treasure]
F - Red Fungus (30 hours forget)               
                                               
The Belly of the Worm                          
  Slimy innards surround you as you find yourself in the belly of the worm.
Several bits hang in your face, forcing you to brush them away in
irritation.  Drawing your hand back quickly, you feel your skin burn
from the slimy substance that covers everything.  With a start, you realize
that it is beginning to eat away at your boots.
                                               
[Exits: none]                                  
The slimy guts of the purple worm surround you.
                                               
<912hp 791m 566mv> gt Damnit, I was swalloed   
Your message didn't get through.               
                                               
A massive shudder goes through the worm's body and you manage to crawl out!
You hear the purple worm's death cry.          
                                               
                                               
                          +--4                 
                          |                    
                          |                    
                          +  3--+              
                          |  |  |

                                +--P--+  +--+  
                                            |  
                                            +  
                                        2   |

                                        |   |\  
D - Deafen fungus (20 hrs deafen)       D-+-+ v 
T - Weak floor trap (fly to avoid)             B 
P - Poisongas
```

```text
                       |
                       + A Ruined Gate in the Palisade
                       |
                       |
                       +
                       |
                       |
    NorthernRoads--+---+



ABANDONED SIEGE ENCAMPMENT (2560)

Nestled against the mountains near Tir-Talath are the remains of a 
formidable siege encampment.  Once home to a clan of misguided lightwalkers, 
they were so blinded by their zealous crusade against a fantasy darkness 
that they did not see true evil approaching.  It would be their brutal 
downfall.

Long abandoned by its original inhabitants, the encampment is now home to an
imperfectly bound evil held at bay only by the vigilant forces of good in an 
epic stalemate.  But be wary, for only intrepid and high-ranked adventurers 
should pass beyond the protective palisade.


WhitecloakEncampment is now archived
```

```text
A  - Arkat's Ale Store
a  - The Holy Altar
B  - The brewery
Ba - The barracks
Bu - The butchery
b  - Before the mines
C  - A dark crevice in a mountain
F  - The armorer's forge
f  - Ferdiad Lane (Ferdiad, a dwarf rager)
G  - The general store
Gr - The Grog and Grub Tavern
H  - The guild of healers
I  - An empty invoker's guild
M  - The center marketplace
O  - Exit from Orc Passage
R  - A rocky canyon
S  - The stone collector's store
Sh - The guild of shamans
T  - An empty transmuter's guild
W  - The Whispering Wind Inn
w  - Wraith (agro)
Wo - A warrior's guild
```

## Ar'atouldain

Status: Current capture
Source: http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=Ar%27atouldain&oldid=5001
Source/capture date: This page was last modified on 8 October 2026, at 23:57.
Content SHA-256: 079ED0B046885BC4166DF194DDA1702328E2FD389D9FC6482CA1E1BF83C0ACE0
Routing status: map transcription only; not playable-route validation.

```text
 Ar'atouldain
 Suggested Levels: 5 - 30
 
 This sign reads: Area written by ShadowDancer, copyright 1994. For use
 exclusively with Carrion Fields or by permission of author. Revised by
 Poetry, copyright 2000.
 Another revision by Mayesha, 2001-2002.

                                             3 Evermoon Hollow
                                             ^     |
                 High Pass-+--+--+--        -+--+--+
                           |  |              |  |  |
              +=+          +-               -3--+--E    E - Elven King (flickering gem)
                |          |                 |  |  |
    Gol'Galath--T          +-                     -+    T - 3 Trolls
                 \         |  |  |  |              |
                  +--+--+--+--+--+--+--B--+--+--+--+    B - Bridge Over the Black River
           Foothills       |  |  |  |              |
                                             2     |    C - Hidden Centaur (with centaur equipment)
                                             ^     |
                                             +    -+_+-C
                                             |  |  |
                                          +--1--+--+
                                             |  |  |
                                       2<-+--2--+--+-Bandit Encampment
                                          |  v
                                          |  1
                                          |
                                     Velkyn Oloth


Maze notes: It is broken up into 4 sectors, nw, sw, ne, se. If
you are in the ne sector, head all east till you hit the edge and go
south to get back to the path. If you are in the sw sector go all west
then north to the path and so on and so forth.

NOTES

Only a maze if you stray from the path. If you head to
Velkyn Oloth, use "Look" to find the right path.

[false direction] but you can also hear the sound of running water
from this direction.
```

```text
               | ^ | |

               | | |                  b: North to a, South to c

 e A   ^       | | |                  d: South to e                         
 s r --d----->-~-~-~-c-<              e: North to d
 t y   ^      /| | | |                g: North (one-way) to f
 e t --f-P >-~-~-~-~-~-e-<            h: North (one-way) to f (All Down, then South to XvartLair)
 r h   |    /| | | | | |              P: Prison of Glymarach
 n   --~---~-~-~-~-~-~-~-g-h-<                                             
       |   | | | | | | | | |
      SH   Coastal Plains         SH: Seantryn Harbor

                      Forward
                    + Cabin 
        Captain's   =
            Cabin +=+  
                    |   Sunken
Deep in the Aryth   |     
                 \  |   Ship
                  +-+ 
        On the      |
      Ocean Floor=+-+
             
Notes

Not a logical map. Going west from a (>-~) will put you on the opposite side of the map (~-<), and vice versa.
If you don't want to think about it, then from the mouth of river by Hamsah, go 2e, 19s, and you're at the Coastal Plains.
The bottom half of the map gets weird, so use the letters as a guide.
```

```text
A - Altar to Yanoreth
C - Conjurer Guild
F - A fox trail
G - North Gate of Barovia
H - A small home
N - Necromancers Guild
n - North Svalich Rd
P - Grundin's Pelts (containers)
p - Pit
R - [Wandering] Sir Tristain (Golden stuff)
S - The Swan Song (food)
Sh - Shaman Guild
T - Thief Guild
W - Warrior Guild
w - Well (water)
AP - Ap Guild
X - Group of good adventurers (Mage, Assassin, and Warrior)

Ghouls appear in forest at night.
Most mobs eat corpses.
```

```text
This object, a beautiful platinum helm, can be referred to as 'platinum dragon helm'.
It has been imbued with a blessing.
Those with a balanced soul cannot use it.
People of a dark heart cannot use it.
It is worth 2500 copper, and is of 26th level of power.
When worn, it protects you against piercing for 8,
bashing for 8, slashing for 8 and magic for 0 points each.
When worn, it affects your ability to do damage by 1 points and your ability to land accurate blows by 1 points.

----------------------------------------
This object, a platinum blade, engraved with the sun, can be referred to as 'platinum blade sun'.
A magical aura surrounds it.
It has been imbued with a blessing.
Those with a balanced soul cannot use it.
People of a dark heart cannot use it.
Only an elf could put it to use.
It is worth 5000 copper, and is of 26th level of power.
It can cause 6d4 points of damage, at average 15.
When worn, it affects your ability to do damage by 5 points and your ability to land accurate blows by 5 points.
```

```text
Notes
* All things in Main Araille map are where things are located in relation to each other, the desert
  itself has a lot of rooms that lead into each other and is not fun to map. If you walk along the
  edge you can find everything except Nomad Village and the Temple, for those, just follow caravan 
  road. A few steps south from the gate you'll see a temple path, and keep following the road down 
  and west for the Village.

Credits
* a rather standard metal shield, it bears some writing, that seems completely untouched by the harsh 
  desert winds....it reads:

    Written by Shokai, for the use of Carrion Fields.
    For levels 5-25, have fun...and consider these bones
    testimony to the consequences of traveling unprepared....
```

## Elemental Temple

Status: Current capture
Source: http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=Elemental_Temple&oldid=5126
Source/capture date: This page was last modified on 9 October 2026, at 04:06.
Content SHA-256: 19600A32059BD75D732C7D1C63040FB9743F3F1605C0841C86A041BAAF752F02
Routing status: map transcription only; not playable-route validation.

```text
                          R   F   E   P   W   X   I                             
                          |   |   |   |   |   |   |                             
                          +   +   +   +   +   +   +                             
                          |   |   |   |   |   |   |                            
                          +-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+                                      
                                      =                                     
                              +   +-+-+-+-+   +                                       
                              |   | | | | |   |                                  
                            +-+-+ +-+-+-+-+ +-+-+                                      
                              |   | | | | |   |                                
                          +   +   +-+-+-+-+   +   +                                  
                          |   |   | | | | |   |   |                            
                        +-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+-+                                     
                          |   |       |       |   |                           
                          +   +       +       +   +                           
                              |       |       |                              
                            +-+-+   F-+-F   +-+-+                                   
                              |       |       |                              
                              S       +       +                              
                                      |                                     
                                Shadow Grove      
                                  
Entrance found on the Shadow Grove map. From the edge of shadow grove
look in each direction following "this way is darker", when you reach
'north west east' intersection go west*2, north*3, east.  Looking east
will confirm 'Something different in this direction.'

Norecall after door to Arch-Bishops

F - Fountain                                                                           
S - Draconian spy (key in the robe)
E - Arch-Bishop Merimar ((G)mace of earth, (G)mantle of earth)
F - Arch-Bishop Magmus ((G)orb of fire, (G)mantle of fire)
R - Arch-Bishop Shiamoor ((G)mantle of frost, (G)mace of frost, (G)orb of ice)
P - Pope Medius (staff of Medius, (G)(H)mantle of Medius, (G)orb of Medius)
W - Arch-Bishop Plantumau ((G)mantle of water, (G)mace of water)
X - Arch-Bishop Czavko ((G)mantle of electricity, (G)mace of electricity, (G)orb of electricity)
I - Arch-Bishop Aeristoma ((G)mantle of air, (G)mace of air, (G)orb of air)

Notes
orb of fire - say ferrinshilt - Grants resist heat.
orb of air  - say aeriloshilt - Grants fly.

Elemental blasts may dispel shields. Medius only seems to dispel.
(G): Glowing
(H): Humming
```

```text
1 &#160;:Inside the first watchtower (fire)
2 &#160;:Inside the second watchtower (fire)
3 &#160;:Inside the third watchtower (fire)
4 &#160;:Inside the fourth watchtower (fire)

C &#160;:To the city in the snow
c &#160;:Site of an old grave.  Look in the directions for a cave entrance, will take you to other cave.

  &#160;:* (Invis)  Duergar Shaman (Helm of Blackened gold, Amulet of the Viper)
  &#160;:* (Hidden) Winter Wolf (Collar of the Winter wolf, Skin of the Winter Wolf?) Use Caution: Frost Blast
F &#160;:Abandoned cabin
   &#160;:* Felar shaman (ceremonial staff, fang-covered skirt, white fur-lined robe draped with animal fangs)
H &#160;:An old farmhouse
I &#160;:Inside an abandoned house (fire) You see: "A small, sad looking house sits here looking about ready to collapse."
i &#160;:Wandering Invoker (Wooden walking staff, banded ring, girdle of many pouches)
L &#160;:Lost in the frigid wastelands
O &#160;:An old farmhouse, (fire) chest (stuff)
R &#160;:Frost Giant Chief (Hide of the Yeti, Jumnarl's double bladed axe, Fur Lined Boots,&#160;?)
S &#160;:A snow worm's lair
  &#160;:* Snow Worm (snow armor-leggings, armguards, torso (limited), shield, large bone (mace), ice-cold brooch (limited)) (no vanish, no gate, fly-only exit)
Sl&#160;:Snow Leopard (skin of the snow leopard)
T &#160;:A trail through the frigid wasteland
W &#160;:The smell of burning firewood drifts to you, but you are unable to locate its source
X &#160;:Lost in the frozen wastes.  Look for "The trampled down snow creates a path that leads to the south." to get to Felar Encampment

== Notes ==

* If there is a fire in the room, you won't be affected by the freezing cold.
* Freezing cold also gives Chill Touch as well as damage.
* Water containers shatter due to the freezing water, unless in another container (like Backpack, large sack, ect.)

== Credits ==

    To the south lies the Frigid Wastelands, stick to the path, and feel free to
    stop into the watchtowers for rest. Any bandit sightings should be reported
    to the trail guard at once.
    
    Frigid Wasteland written by Lloth Oct-1996.
    Minor formatting update by Valguarnera Dec 2001.
```

```text
It seems some horrible error on the part of a usually reliable and confidential 
messenger service has caused a letter to be sent to you by mistake. It reads: 

"My Liege, 

I have discovered a small pocket of the lawless, hidden in a small but deep 
forest north of South Sutherspring Road. Infiltration has gone smoothly. 
It seems they are lead by a witch, who has managed to ensorcel the entire 
forest, making it impossible to stumble upon their village. But I have 
discovered subtle markings that show a trail which leads to a wall of mist. 
Find the markings by examining everything carefully, even the holes within 
the trunks of the dead trees. Such is not possible without the light of day. 
Passing through the mist undoubtedly takes you into their home. From there, 
avoid directions which fill you with foreboding, and you should remain safe 
from her deluding enchantment. I am preparing directions through the forest 
and a map of their homes using the usual codes. 

Your Servant, 
Y "
```

Status: Current capture

```text
NOTE: Below is the path into the little town area which begins at "E".
There are exits in all directions, but only the path is drawn.

           Octagonal Tower  The Ancient Road to the Wastes
                    |              |
           @       MAZE2           +
           |        |              |
     +     + +     MAZE1           +
     |     |/       |              |
  +--+--+  +     +  |        +--+--+
  |     |  |     |  |        |     
  E--+  +--+--+--+--+        +--+    
  |           |                 |
  |           W                 |
  |                             |
  +--------------+--------------+     

 E - Entrance to the town
 W - Wizard
                 
 MAZE1 - A Thick Pine Forest - try nnns to the exit room (can't remember if its random or not)
         To get through, look for - The forest looks like it gets thinner in this direction.
         To get out, look for - A faint path appears in this direction.

 MAZE2 - A Thinning Pine Forest - nnnen from one exit to the next exit room (can't remember if its random or not)
         to leave, The forest looks like it gets thicker here.
         to enter octagonal tower, There seems to be a small trail this way.
         Just one step north from the entrace, you can find the Huge Dire Wolf under a log.
```

```text
X = Sleep trap (look in the corpse and you fall asleep)
L = felar sentry (ivory tail ring, leather hindpaws with bone tipped claws,
                  leather forepaws with bone tipped claws)
F = fire giants (nacreous mask of lizard skin, glistening dagger, 
                 decorated mask of lizard skin)
R = Artifact room (gnome with robe covered in glowing runes)
B = Bazaar
A = Arial guard (wandering) (flail wing braces claws red armor)
K = (invis) Khruvnil (long black robe,&#160;?)
C = dwarven captain
  a dwarven captain is using:
  <worn on body>      a suit of mithril armor
  <worn on legs>      a set of thick mithril leggings
  <worn on feet>      a set of wide mithril boots
  <worn as shield>    a burnished shield
  <wielded>           a wide mithril battle axe

S = Duergar scouts
a duergar scout is using:
<worn around neck>  a darkened mithril necklace
<worn on body>      a chainmail shirt of darkened mithril
<worn on head>      a darkened mithril helm
<worn on legs>      a pair of darkened mithril leggings
<worn on arms>      a pair of darkened mithril sleeves
<worn as shield>    a darkened mithril buckler
<wielded>           a bloody flail of darkened mithril
<wielded>           (Glowing) the broken remains of a flaming sword
<wielded>           a twin-bladed darkened mithril poleaxe
```

```text
Notes
Three parts to the forest, two mazes at each end and the middle part that isn't a maze.

Heading in towards the middle of the forest, look for:
A thin animal trail leads further into the forest.
The trail begins to widen in this direction.

Heading out towards either edge of the forest, look for:
The forest appears to be thinning.
You can barely make out what appears to be the edge of this forest.
The path seems to lead to the edge of the trees.

If you get lost you only have to wander around until you can see them again.
```

```text
TARGETH TARGETHS MANOR 'TARGETHS MANOR'
After a lifetime of war, one of Thera's most experienced mercenaries decided to
find lands of his own to rule, rather than fight the wars of others.  Finding
fertile lands beyond the Shadow Peaks and with a personal army, the mercenary
Captain Targeth easily displaced the local Lord.  Though wary of the many
guards which now patrol the manor's lands, the daily life of the peasantry
remains mostly unchanged as they toil among the fields.  Lord Targeth himself
is said to be a firm master, though not unfair to his newfound populace.

Rebuilding the Manorhouse in a more defensible location, the serf village has
also seen improvements to better provide the basic necessities to the populace.
Travelers are warned to keep out of the fields at night as raiders from the
nearby Fhalaugash Keep are said to roam the darkness.


******************************************************************************
```

```text
R - Ranger
s - Supply Depot (general store)
S - Sleeping Quarters
t - Tower of Tir-Talath
T - Transmuter
u - Wells, 'use well' twice then 'drink well'
v - Vegetable Stand
W - Warrior Guild
w - Weaponsmith's
z - Free Wine!!! =]
Z - Zardroff (Alchemist)

0 - ground level  (can go up)
1 - one level up  (can go down (and up if in tower or keep))
2 - two levels up (can go down)

Within the vast northern plains region south of the Udgaard Mountains, 
several farms and agricultural communities are nestled. Rising up in 
the midst of the vast, sweeping grasslands, is the Tir-Talath Outpost. 
Serving as a common center of commerce, defense, and study, Tir-Talath 
is a formidable fortress in a land often plagued by bandits, natural 
disasters, and an aggressive neighboring territory. 

Visitors are welcome in Tir-Talath, which boasts several amenities in a 
region otherwise limited to rather rural conditions. A temple in service 
to the wise and neutral gods of the Theran pantheon offers healing services 
and sanctums for prayer, and the open barracks of the Tir-Talath militia 
welcome any visitors who wish to study the art of war within. The majestic 
Tower of Tir-Talath offers a place of magical study for both mages and 
amateurs alike, while the sights and smells of the farmer's market are sure 
to delight the sense of anyone seeking a unique farm grown gift or treat. 

Order in Tir-Talath is kept by the local militia, who defend the northern 
plains region and its people under the high command of Baron Telenius, a 
reluctant, but well-trusted patron who rarely grants audiences to visitors. 
His personal keep, however, is open to visitors, and stands proudly in the 
heart of the outpost on top of a secure cistern from which the outpost draws 
water into its four wells.
```

```text
The Plains of Udgaard was rewritten by Yean for exclusive use by Carrion
Fields, LLC in 2007.  No distribution in any form is permitted without
prior consent.  Thanks to all who aided with this project, especially to
Lyristeon, Rayihn, Ysaloerye and Kastellyn, for their patient mentorship,
```

```text
* Transportation spells don't work.
* Roaming aggro mobs (cavefishers, dark-elf patrols).

D - Debris (ghastly remains)
M - Miggen the Svirfneblin Mycologist
G - Guardian
L - Kteng's Lab
= - hidden tunnel, medium size or smaller only
A - Aran'gird
```

```text
       |             |/|//: |
       +=D           + +  z=+-+
 T     " |             |    |
 O-+-+-+-+-----+       +    |
   |   |       |      /     |
 E-+-+-+       +   +-+      |
 M |  \        |  /         +
 E-+   \       +-+     \   /
 R      \     /| |      +-+ 
 A       +-+-+ +-+
 L         | |
 D         +-+

Key:
= or " or // is a door or something that is barring your way (= is e/w, " is n/s, // is u/d).
This only applies to things that cannot be opened using the "open" command.
C You can crawl north to c, a one way trip
D You can crawl north to d, a one way trip 
L is for Lever
M is for Menhir
x You can crawl north to y, or south to z from here
y You can crawl south to x (Can crawl west when gnome sized)+
z You can crawl north to x (Can crawl west when gnome sized)+
W Gnome selling wands (invis).
S Gnome selling scrolls.
X Gnome selling maps.
If too large get the echo:
'You push with all your might, but still can't squeeze through the window'.
```

## Whistlewood Swamp

Status: Current capture
Source: http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=Whistlewood_Swamp&oldid=5070
Source/capture date: This page was last modified on 9 October 2026, at 03:43.
Content SHA-256: 9991C6C7F7A2A9E740BB2A93317C3210C9AF449565BA3245FECF3D7AADBEB9AA
Routing status: map transcription only; not playable-route validation.

```text
Whistlewood Swamp
Suggested Levels: 40-51

Created for Carrion Fields by Shane H. W. Travis. This area cannot be used
elsewhere without express permission from the author. Thanks to all the
immortals of Carrion Fields for their insight, input, and patience with my
many questions. Special thanks to Douglas Carney for his *massive* effort
on my behalf, and to David Tuggle and Doug Stoll for their encouragement
and contributions both tangible and intangible.

               P  S
               |  |  |
             --H--H--H-E-GlaurukSpawningGround
               |  |
                  |  Aratouldain
                  |  |  
                  +--D--CoastalPlains
                  |  |
                  J  Silverwood
                
                
                
     |  |  |       
   --H--H--H--         
     |  |  |  | 
     +--+--+--H--          
     |  |  |  |
     +--+--+--H--     This part is not maze. "H" means this is where the maze begins.
     |     |  |  | 
     +     +--+--H--     
     |     |  |  |  
Maltrakis  +--+ 
           |     
           J
           |     
           +    


E - At the Edge of Whistlewood Swamp
H - In the Heart of Whistlewood Swamp
J - Jara Silverstaff (a mottled gray-green wool cloak (about), a hand-tooled leather belt (),
                      the Axe of Dissolution, a metal-rimmed shield, everfull flask)
    a finely-made display case (a silver staff)
    a stout wooden chest ()

S - Shaman of Maltrakis - (a phylactery of potence, the Wrath of Mal'trakis)
P - Oneheart priest - (a phylactery of fortitude)
D - wizened old man (a phylactery of free movement, a thornspike staff - wielded)

Aggressive purebloods at the swamp. Using spears and impaling.
 A manor infested with rats beyond the swamp?

Paths to different NPCs seem easiest found by room landmarks, but there is no sense of
direction given, so finding the path is a matter of trial and error. This is really easy
with a familiar. Not so easy without one.

There are a few set paths, however, and all use the first step into the 
swamp on the east side as the starting point.

Path to Chosen One Healer - swwwnnn
1. golden web, glittering in the light (this is the entrance to the swamp) - go south one
2. A water hickory grows here - w
3. muskrat lodge - w
4. explain these scorch-marks... - w
5. The water is still here - n
6. No wonder, then, that the whole swamp seems alive - n
7. golden mouse sitting very quietly on a tree trunk - n to the healer

Path to Jara Silverstaff - swssswsws
(Move to each of these landmarks consecutively, then south, west, south.)
1. golden web, glittering in the light (this is the entrance to the swamp) - s
2. A water hickory grows here - w
3. muskrat lodge - s
4. a beautiful copper-winged butterfly - s
5. brightly striped snake - s
6. A gray owl - w
7. You could easily climb the southern rise - s
8. w
9. s

Path to Shaman (probably. this is also the easiest to stumble upon) (from one south of healer it can be (maybe) ew then nn to shaman)
1. golden web, glittering in the light (this is the entrance to the swamp)
2. easier to get lost
3. and... and... and...
4. you discover a clump of saltweed
5. an insidious fungus
6. north from the above room

Path to Druid (in progress)
1. wander the lavendar until you look all and see: "The swamp gets shallower, but is filled with rushes and cattails."s
2. could be a leftover from the effects of the flowers - look all shows "The swamp gets shallower this way." - e
3. aggro razor weed is the next room - e
4. s to get attacked by the razorweed, then s to the strangleweed.
5. e to get attacked by the strangleweed, then e to the druid.
```

## Waters of the Deep

Status: Current capture
Source: http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=Waters_of_the_Deep&oldid=5089
Source/capture date: This page was last modified on 9 October 2026, at 03:56.
Content SHA-256: 7FB0043B7614F58EC605CBFD618F5B644611B842ADA0E71F411A4E8852C3C1E3
Routing status: map transcription only; not playable-route validation.

```text
Waters of the Deep, suggested levels 15 - 50

        DragonSea
             \     
              \
            U--+   <----Here is the entrance under the docks, visible from HamsahMutaz
             \          Another entrance is the Whirlpool in the ArythOcean
              +          Note: Whirlpool and Waters of the Deep are the same area,
               \               so I've consolidated them.
                +
                 \
            S--+--+
             \     \
          C1--+     \              
                    maze
                      \
                  The Landing


                    +     +--+        +
                    |     |  |        |\
           +--+--+--+--+--+--+--G--+--K C2
          /      =  |     |  |        |
         /       +  +     +--+        +
        /        |           |
       +     A   +           +         
       |     |               |
       +--+--I--+--+--+      +
             =        |
          +--+--+     |
             |        |
             +        |
             |        |
             |        |
             |        |
             |      a |
             |      ^\|
             |      | +  ArythOcean
             |      +          /
             |      |         v      
             |      M--+     +
             |      |  ^    /
             |      +  |   v
             |         +  +
             |         ^ /
             |         |v
             |         +        +--+
             |                 /| /|
             |/               +--+ |
      +------+--------+  +    | +| +
      |/     |        |/ |    |/ |/ Cage
      +------+--------+--+--+=+--+ 
      |      |/       |  |
      +--The landing--+  +
             |
             |
             |
             |
          T--+--W  Holding Pen  H  H
             |     |            |  |
             +--+--+------------+--+--H
             |     |            |  |
             |     Holding Pen  H  H
             |
   Chamber --+-- Unholy Chamber
             |   Priest (a small pouch)
             |
         A Shrine

U - under the docks, visible from HamsahMutaz
S - An enormous sea serpent (Serpent-scale armor)
C1 - Silver chest (treasures)
T - Tailor sahuagin (a seashell dagger)
W - weaponsmith (a bone-tipped spear, a coral dagger)
H - A Common House
a - Air chamber
M - Muddy Intersection
A - Altar/ Shrine. You can recall from here.
I - An Intersection.
G - Captain of the guards (in the pouch he wears, there is a key to unlock the door from whirlpool to waters of the deep).
K - Sahuagin King (Cloak of the Deep, spear of sahuagin, fine coral necklace, talon of the Sahuagin King)
    sahuagin priestess (ring of coral, shell necklace, small stone dagger).
C2 - chest (bone bracelet, 15 gold)
```

## The Xvart Lair

Status: Current capture
Source: http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=The_Xvart_Lair&oldid=5117
Source/capture date: This page was last modified on 9 October 2026, at 04:03.
Content SHA-256: 9EB0F89E5172233FF59628FFB7DD803B55355B179CAF732C5FF7617BE85A7775
Routing status: map transcription only; not playable-route validation.

```text
You see some words carved into a smooth rock by the wall.
 
Area discovered by Pico, Implementor of Thera, September, 1999.

Suggested levels:  10 - 25

                                                                                   C        
                                                                                  /
                                       +----T----+           +            [A]    B--L
                                      /          |           |    +       /      |
                            +--O--+--+           |   P--P----+   /|      +       +
                            |  |        +-----+  +   |  |    / [A]|     /        |
Ocean Floor of             SPN—P        |    /   |   |  |   +-----+    +----+----PT
ArythOcean                  |  |        W   +----+   |  |
      |                  +--+--+        |   |    /   |  |    T         CoastalPlains
      +    +            /        R*--+--+   O   +----P--PT  /|                 |
      |   /| +         /                    |              / P--P              +
      +--+ |/|     +--+----CS               +--B--O--O----+  |  |        +--+ /
           U |       /     |                      |          P--P--+    /   |/ 
         R2--+      /      O                      +                |   /    +
             |  T--+       |        S--X          |                +--+
             + /   |  +    S--+    /              +              
             |/    + /|       |   /               |
             +     |/ +--+    +--+                +--R1
                   T     |    |  |
                         R    S--+ 
                                /
                               /
                              J
                              |
                              Y


B – Boulder
C – Xvart Chief (tigerseye pendant, compact iron flail)
    w/ Lockbox (Trapped[Poison for 20 hrs] - carved garnet, silver bar, small pile of
                 copper coins, tiny blue-white diamond, pale blue gem
                 sparkling green gem)
J – Jade Elf Youth (pair of crude chains, pair of crude chains)
L – Xvart Lieutenant (hatchet)
N – Gold Nugget – Shock Trap
O – Stocky Xvart (small, wide-bladed sword)
P – Pool – Poisoned Water (no – Stat)
R – Xvart Raider(x2) (dark brown greatcloak, short rapier, burlap sack)
    Xvart Raid Leader (dark brown greatcloak, officer's insignia armbands,
                       black steel sword)
R1- a leather knapsack: invitation from the Empress,map of Western
                        Seantryn Modan, map of Eastern Seantryn Modan
R2- Seaweed
S – Slim Xvart (small leather pouch, small iron dagger)
T – Stern-Looking Xvart (short rapier)
U – Underwater
W - Water
X – Xvart Shaman (small jade ring, black satin robe, platinum bracelet
                  set with numerous jewels, copper shortsword)
    w/ Lockbox (an etched and pitted elven shortsword, white silk purse,
                little ivory icon, jade figurine)
Y – Beaten Young Lady (worn silk dress, pair of crude chains, pair of
                       crude chains)

Evil cousins to the kobolds, these strange creatures have taken up 
residence in a warren of caves and caverns somewhere around Seantryn 
Modan. None are really sure where the entrances lie, but the xvart 
have been known to pop up and conduct lightning quick raids in several 
places, including the jade mountains and the Aryth Ocean. They make 
off with gold, jewelry, armor and even people! Hardier than kobolds, 
the xvart are very protective of their lair, and will attack intruders 
on sight.
```

## Ysigrath

Status: Current capture
Source: http://wiki.qhcf.net/index.php?title=Ysigrath&oldid=5062
Source/capture date: This page was last modified on 9 October 2026, at 03:42.
Content SHA-256: 001226C7D00F66D831630D7119B8011DBEBE2EBB3DD6C72ECB01D9A05DAFFF0E
Routing status: map transcription only; not playable-route validation.

```text
Ysigrath is no summon, no gate, and no word. (except from Hallowed place)

                HU WH U  WH HU
|--------|      |  |  |  |  |    Sz'weh
|        |------+--+--+--+--+    Village
|        |      |  |  |  |  |
|        |      HU HU |  HU HU
|        |            |
|        |         HU-+-HU
|        |            |   =
|        |            |    + a dark place(gems)
|        |         HU-+-HU
|        |            |
| Heart  |   |--------------------|
|  of    |   |untamed wilds of    |
|Ysigrath|   |the Ysigrath.(Maze2)|
|(Maze3) |   |--------------------|
|        |            |
|        |         DW-+-DW
|        |            |
|        |         DW-+-DW   K'sra
|        |            |      Village
|        |  DW DW CD  |  S
|        |  |  |  |   |  |
|        |--+--+--+---C--H
|--------|  |  |  |   |
            DW DW DW  |
                      |
                  DW--+--DW
                      |                  SeaOfDespair
                  DW--K--DW  The Forgotten  | |   |
                      |             Island  ~-~---~  
              |----------------|          ^  \|   |    (Note: This part of the Sea of Despair
              |Ysigrath (Maze1)|--T--D--Dock--~---~          and Yisgrath are same area)
              |----------------|          |   |   |           
                                        AshesOfNoWhere


C - center of the village of the K'sra
CD - chieftain's dwelling, Ghyss (lichen gloves)
D - dark trail into Ysigrath
DW - Inside a K'sra dwelling
H - Hallowed Place (Sanctified room)
HU - Inside a Sz'weh hut
O - On Dock
S - Shaman's Dwelling, Jhel'nra (fragrant necklace of marsh flowers)
T - trail's end
U - unholy place. Zen'kra (Silver Chain Flail(FB) Mithril Chain Flail(AB), unholy symbol)
WH - Inside a Sz'weh warrior's hut (Bone Axe, Bone Armor)
~ - Sea of Despair

Maze1:
Into K'sra&#160;: 
Look for "Something seems different about the swamp in this direction.". The direction is north.
path:   sw nnw n   from the first room off the main trail (room 1). The first sw to room 6 can be replaced with nnw, see, nwe

Into K'sra from any room of Maze1:
Mindlessly spam nnw until you get there. Finding room 1 and following above route works as well.

Back to AshesOfNoWhere:   
eee, wse, swe or ssee   From the first room south of K'Sra Entrance (room 9). Last e is from room 1 to T.

Back to AshesOfNoWhere from any Maze1 room: 
-Look east for "You think you see a trail." If you see it, you are in room 1. Do this every time you enter a room.
-If you are not in room 1, go south twice. 
-If you still haven't reached room 1, you are either in room 4 or room 8. Go west once. If you aren't in room 1 by now, go north to room 1.

Map of Maze1:

    K 6 7
    | ^ v
  2<9<5>2    
    v ^ |
1<1<4<7>1-T
    | ^ |
  9>8<6-3-8>1
    | v |
    4 4<2>2

In above map, | and - are two-way exits, <>^v are one way exits. A>B is a one-way exit from A to B to the east. 
All 9 rooms of the maze have 4 exits, all of which cannot be shown in the above map. Hence, exits by room are listed below. 
Exits in below map all are one-way from the room in the center of the cross. The map seen above is based on map seen below.

  2     3     1     8     6     7     5     4     K
  |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |
1-1-T 4-2-2 6-3-8 1-4-6 9-5-2 8-6-3 4-7-1 3-8-1 2-9-8
  |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |
  3     1     2     8     4     4     2     4     4

Maze2:
path to Sz'weh From K'Sra: nenwwn From first room north of K'Sra
Into Sz'weh: Look for "You can barely see smoke rising from the swamp in this direction."
Into K'sra from Sz'Weh:   wes    From first room south of Sz'weh Entrance

Maze3: [Poisonous gas]
A Lost Paladin?&#160;: Carries Silverbladed Poleaxe (Wrath Attack), Platinum Signet Ring
A swamp serpent carries a platinum signet ring.

Into K'sra  &#160;: Look for "You can see the village of the K'sra."
Into Sz'weh &#160;: Look for "You see the village of the Sz'weh."
To Pyrohydra&#160;: Look for "an area filled with black moss." [Forget] Then "some more swamp." 
                Then "a partially-concealed cave entrance."
                Pyrfohydra carries a pyrohydra-scale shield and a necklace of pyrohydra teeth 
                (EQ BURNER!)
To hatchlings: Look for "You see swamp." Then "You see a cave."
                Black dragon hatchling carries a belt of black dragonscales, black dragon's tooth (dagger), 
                and piece of black dragon scales
To Druid guild: Look for "A circle of stones rises out of the swamp."

Quick Reference
Maze 1 To: swnnwn
Maze 1 From: swe

Maze 2 To: nenwwn
Maze 2 From: wes
```
